金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

### Page 4

3
AI 算力需求推升科技業者資本支出，帶動資料中心長期擴建
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
預估全球AI Workload容量需求將由2025年44GW成長至2030年156GW，CAGR達28.8%，五年新增112GW。顯示AI算力
需求將持續帶動資料中心建置及相關基礎設施投資。
預估北美主要雲端與科技業者(CSP)資本支出將由2025年4,450億美元成長至2028年1.15兆美元，CAGR達37.2%。隨CSP持
續投入算力擴建，將帶動AI伺服器、機櫃、電力及散熱等基礎設施需求，支撐相關供應鏈中長期成長動能。
AI算力需求驅動資料中心擴建，2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW CSP資本支出加速擴張，2028年規模上看1.15兆美元
資料來源：McKinsey & Company、兆豐投顧整理

AIDC 擴建推升用電規模與機櫃密度，供電及散熱能力影響建置進度
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
電網擴建與資料中心建置存在時程落差 ，電力取得速度成為AIDC擴張的重要限制。IEA指出，新建電網從規劃、許可至完工
可能需5–15年，資料中心建置則約1–3年。
AI運算朝高密度系統整合，配電與液冷升級需求涵蓋GPU及CSP自研ASIC。除NVIDIA GB200／GB300外，Google TPU 8t
／8i採第四代液冷，AWS Trainium3 Teton3 MAX採冷板液冷架構，AMD Helios亦已有支援最高246kW雙寬機櫃的合作參
考設計，系統驗證與量產交付能力將成為AIDC建置之關鍵。
全球再生能源與大型儲能併網排隊規模逾2,300 GW 高密度GPU與ASIC機櫃功率上升將持續推動配電及液冷升級
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 6

5
電網補強與機房建置存在時程落差，提升AIDC現地供電需求
➢ AI算力需求與AIDC建置瓶頸
AIDC擴張需同步取得發電供應、輸配電容量及場址受電能力，任一環節受限，皆可能影響機房投運時程。隨單一場址用電規
模擴大，部分專案需新增變電站 、饋線及相關設備，供電進度因而受到重電設備交期拉長 、區域電網併網與饋線容量不足 、
熱損耗與轉換效率等因素影響。
NLR指出，美國資料中心建置與負載爬坡通常約需12–24個月，公用事業電網補強則常需3–5年。我們認為，兩者的時程落差
將提高業者評估分階段供電及現地發電的意願 ，並為燃料電池與燃氣機組等方案創造導入機會；實際效益仍須綜合評估燃料 、
許可、可靠度與完整供電成本。
AIDC供電涉及多個環節，場址接電能力影響投運時程 資料中心(AIDC)建置與電網補強時程比較

### Page 7

6
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC提供模組化連續供電選項
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
當電網補強時程無法配合機房投運 ，業者可評估現地發電，搭配分階段送電與儲能等方式銜接供電需求 。方案選擇須綜合考
量連續供電能力、燃料供應、建置條件與可靠度要求。
Bloom Energy採用固態氧化物燃料電池(SOFC)，透過電化學反應將燃料轉換為電力，可依需求配置模組，提供持續性現地
電源。其應用可涵蓋與電網並聯或微電網架構，實際部署仍須配合燃料、許可及場內電力系統設計。
現地發電增加機房供電來源 現地發電與儲能方案比較
資料來源：Bloom Energy、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：DOE、Cummins、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理
註：本圖簡化供電路徑，電力轉換、UPS、儲能與備援配置依案場設計
方案 主要功能 選用時的評估重點
電池儲能 電力銜接 與功率調節 儲能量體、輸出功率及充電來源
柴油發電機 停電備援 依額定選擇運轉用途 燃料補給、設備額定及適用許可
燃氣渦輪 可配置為 持續發電來源 天然氣供應與工程、運轉條件

SOFC燃料電池 模組化配置 持續發電 燃料供應、高溫啟停及電堆維護
註：實際用途依額定與案場設計；天然氣SOFC仍有CO2排放

### Page 8

7
SOFC以電化學反應直接發電，模組化整合支援資料中心現地供電
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
BE採固態氧化物燃料電池 (SOFC)，氧離子穿過固態電解質與燃料進行電化學反應直接發電 ，電子經外部迴路流動 ，形成直
流電，不經燃燒，電效率達 53–65%。SOFC由多片電池組成電堆 ，再整合為發電模組，搭配燃料處理、空氣供應、熱管理
與電力轉換設備，形成完整供電系統。
一座1GW的資料中心採用濕式冷卻燃氣渦輪機聯合循環發電系統 ，在其使用壽命期間，大約會消耗250億加侖的水；但若是
用燃料電池僅需140萬加侖。
氧離子穿越陶瓷電解質，電子經外部迴路形成電力 電池片逐級整合為電堆、發電模組與供電系統
資料來源：IEA、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：AEP、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 9

8
發電效率與部署條件，決定SOFC在AIDC的採用機會
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
AIDC持續運轉將累積大量燃料支出 ，提高發電效率可降低相同電力輸出所需的天然氣量 。SOFC具高效率發電特性，其經濟
價值須結合燃料價格、使用時數及維護成本評估。
SOFC、燃氣引擎與燃氣渦輪各有部署及運轉條件 。業主須共同評估設備交付 、場址工程、燃氣供應、排放許可及維運需求 ，
才能判斷適合的現地供電方案。
資料來源：兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：DOE、GE、Caterpillar、BE、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 10

9
政策支援燃料電池投資，木星計畫燃氣與許可牽動落地時程
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
木星計畫位於美國新墨西哥州 ，為Oracle支援OpenAI Stargate建置的AI資料中心園區。Oracle於2026年4月宣布，規劃採
用最高2.45GW的Bloom Energy燃料電池裝置容量，取代原先燃氣輪機與柴油發電機方案，展現對SOFC現地供電的需求。
木星計畫的SOFC方案仍以天然氣供應為前提，燃氣接入與空氣品質許可均影響建置進度。州土地辦公室7月維持拒發特定管
線通行權等申請，Oracle於10月另提出微電網設計補件，顯示大型專案須同步協調設備建置、燃料供應與案場許可。
美國 OBBBA 將合格燃料電池納入 Section 48E 投資稅額抵減 甲骨文木星計劃微電網建置須經許可審查與方案調整
資料來源：GPO、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源：NMED、NMSLO、Oracle、兆豐投顧整理
項目 內容
適用制度 Section 48E 合格燃料電池專屬條款
抵減比例 合格投資基礎金額 x 30%

排放條件 專屬條款排除一般設施的溫室氣體排放資格門檻
加成限制 此項30%比例不得疊加其他加成

### Page 11

10
大型AIDC合作案擴大，BE交付與擴產節奏成為供應鏈觀察重點
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
Oracle、Nebius等業者已公告採用BE燃料電池，顯示SOFC已取得大型AIDC商業合作。評估需求規模時，須分別辨識合作
上限、已簽約容量及實際部署進度。
機組製造、場址工程與許可程序須共同推進。供應鏈需求應依BE製造擴產、設備出貨及案場交付節奏追蹤，並將融資合作框
架與設備訂單分開評估。
BE大型合作案涵蓋設備採購、供電服務與專案融資 BE製造產能隨現地供電需求擴張，2027年起每年增加逾2GW

### Page 12

11
SOFC擴建帶動高溫熱管理與電力線纜需求，台廠切入不同供應環節
➢ SOFC現地供電與台廠供應鏈
依Ceres基於BNEF資料之分析 ，2030年全球SOFC市場機會約22GW，其中資料中心應用占 49%(約10.8GW)，為最大單一
應用市場。隨AI資料中心擴建與供電需求提升 ，資料中心可望成為SOFC商業化的重要需求來源 ，帶動高溫熱管理與電力零
組件的應用機會。
SOFC系統需要高溫熱交換 、電力轉換與系統配線等零組件 。高力提供熱反應器 、複熱器等高溫熱管理關鍵零組件；宏致則
供應高溫反應模組耐高溫電纜、電力控制與轉換模組內電纜，以及系統管理與電力傳輸線纜。
Ceres預估2030年全球SOFC市場機會達22GW，其中資料中心占49% 325 kW Energy Server 的組件與運作方式

### Page 13

12
供電與液冷協同升級，支撐AIDC算力密度與能源效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
在總供電及機房空間受限下，AIDC須兼顧能源與空間使用效率。降低冷卻與配電等基礎設施耗能，可在相同總用電條件下提
高IT設備用電占比；評估整體效益時，除PUE外，仍須觀察單位運算耗電及單位面積算力。
液冷透過冷板、分歧管與CDU將熱帶至設施端，高壓直流與Power sidecar則調整供電及設備配置。隨散熱需求延伸至電源
模組，供電與冷卻系統須協同設計 ，兼顧電力轉換、散熱耗能及部署密度。而電源模組採用液冷，全新的水冷資料中心機房
即不再需要傳統的冷熱通道(Hot/Cold Aisle)與大型空調系統，基礎設施CAPEX預估可節省高達10%。
相同總用電下，降低基礎設施耗能可提高IT用電占比 運算與電源同步液冷，降低對機房氣流散熱的依賴
資料來源：DOE、兆豐投顧試算 資料來源：NVIDIA、MEGMEET、兆豐投顧整理
度/電
註：依PUE定義進行情境試算，非特定液冷方案實測結果。IT設備用

電包含伺服器內部風扇等耗電，不等同晶片運算用電。

熱捕捉範圍擴大，液冷設計由主晶片延伸至整機架構
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
除CPU、GPU、TPU及ASIC等主要熱源外，液冷進一步涵蓋DIMM、OSFP光模組與板級元件，透過冷板及導熱板提高液體
帶走熱量的比例，降低對氣流散熱的依賴。
為支援不同熱源與機構配置 ，運算主板與整機伺服器之液冷解決方案多元化 ，包括冷板迴路式、混合式及整合式大冷板架構 。
我們認為，隨液冷涵蓋元件增加，流道與連接設計、模組組裝及可靠度驗證的重要性將提高 ，供應鏈機會亦由單顆冷板延伸
至整機冷卻整合。
液冷涵蓋主晶片與周邊元件，熱捕捉比例最高達100% 冷板迴路式、混合式與整合式架構支援不同整機配置
資料來源：CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理 資料來源： CoolIT、兆豐投顧整理

### Page 15

CDU串接IT與設施冷卻迴路，承接液冷擴張後的排熱需求
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及板級元件，液體承擔的設備熱量增加；機櫃功率密度提高 ，更進一步推升液冷
迴路的排熱需求。CDU串接IT側與設施側，透過熱交換器、循環泵浦與控制系統維持供液條件 ，其換熱能力與供液穩定性將
成為高密度AIDC的重要設計環節。
CDU升級除提高散熱容量，更須兼顧能源與空間使用效率 。OCP 2026的2.3MW系統模擬研究顯示，在相同散熱負載下，比
較方案間全年冷卻系統能耗差距約17%。
冷板取熱、CDU換熱、設施端排熱，構成完整液冷迴路 相同2.3MW散熱負載之下，CDU選型仍牽動流量與全年能耗

案場溫度。

Deschutes開放2MW級CDU設計，瞄準能源與空間效率提升
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
Google將第五代 CDU設計Deschutes貢獻予OCP，提供高密度 AI機房可採用的開放規格 。其v1.0設計以2MW散熱容量 、

耗能，須從整體冷卻系統評估其能源與空間效益。

800VDC重整供電配置，液冷延伸至高密度電源櫃
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
隨AIDC機櫃功率提高，低電壓供電面臨大電流、導體體積與電源設備占用空間等挑戰。Power sidecar將前端電力轉換與部
分備援設備移至獨立電源櫃 ，再以800VDC送至運算櫃，並依架構於負載附近降壓；透過提高輸電電壓與調整設備配置 ，支
援更高功率密度部署。
NVIDIA第二代MGX Power Rack參考設計，朝100%液冷發展，並標示880kW供電能力(N配置、含BBU)及90秒備援供電時
間。隨電力轉換與備援設備集中，液冷需求將進一步延伸至電源模組，使供電與散熱成為能源及空間效率升級的重要環節。
前端電源移至獨立機櫃，800VDC支援高功率密度供電 NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃朝全液冷發展，液冷散熱模組與零組件用量提升

液冷導入電源模組，推進功率密度與能源效率升級
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
電源模組導入液冷後，可透過冷板將功率元件的熱量帶至冷卻液 ，降低對風扇、大型散熱鰭片與風道的需求 。相較風冷設計，
液冷有助縮小散熱結構占用空間，並降低風扇耗電及元件工作溫度，使電源模組朝更高功率密度發展。
MEGMEET於2026年10月OCP展示100kW、800V液冷AC/DC電源層，採2RU不含BBU設計，產品標示轉換效率逾98%；另
展示1RU、95kW的800V轉54V液冷DC/DC電源層。我們認為，液冷應用延伸至整流與降壓環節 ，將擴大電源冷板、流道設
計及模組整合的需求，提升水冷板、快接頭、滑軌、機殼與液冷busbar之需求量。
水冷板承接功率元件熱量，降低電源模組對風扇與風道的依賴 液冷電源層涵蓋前端整流與櫃內降壓，支援緊湊供電配置

晶片TDP與出貨量同步提升，水冷板技術與ASP仍具升級空間
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片TDP逐代提升，NVIDIA由GB300的1.4kW增至Rubin 2.3kW、Rubin Ultra約2.6kW，TPU v9與Trainium4將達3~4kW。

顆、占比由37%升至55%，超越GPU；出貨放量加上電源櫃與光模組導入液冷，水冷板用量與滲透率仍有提升空間。
TDP持續攀升，水冷板技術與ASP仍有升級空間 AI晶片出貨量預估，整體供應鏈於2027年仍供不應求

冷板與流體零組件升級，須兼顧散熱效能與系統耗電
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
晶片功耗提升推高冷板流量需求 ，惟CDU可供IT側使用的壓差有限(Deschutes規格為80~90psi)，冷板與快接頭須以更低壓
降輸送更大流量。Rubin Ultra將導入內建球閥、提升流量的ZQD；我們認為，快接頭技術門檻由防漏延伸至大流量低壓降，
帶動規格與單價升級。
NVIDIA MGX第二代電源櫃全面液冷，以1座電源櫃搭配2座運算櫃為一組試算，每組新增26片電源冷板及52顆快接頭；惟兩
段電源轉換熱損合計僅約供電量4%，增量以件數為主，價值量約為運算櫃快接頭的5%。
電源設備液冷化：每組(1*電源櫃+2*運算櫃)零組件用量試算 流量提升推動快接頭規格升級，Rubin Ultra每tray 10顆MQD改採ZQD

### Page 22

21
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，單櫃價值倍增帶動台廠受惠
➢ 液冷系統擴張與AIDC效率升級
液冷由CPU、GPU延伸至記憶體、光模組及電源設備，Rubin世代全面採用100%液冷，CDU容量放大至2.3~3MW。我們預
估，單櫃液冷價值(含CDU)由約10萬美元倍增至約20萬美元，全球液冷市場將由2026年105億美元成長至2030年853億美元
(CAGR約69%)。
價值升級沿三條主線：CDU放大容量與泵浦冗餘、冷板導入雙面水冷與鑽石銅、快接頭朝大口徑低壓降及ZQD升級；電源櫃
液冷則以件數貢獻增量。我們認為，冷板與快接頭兼具規格升級與用量成長，奇鋐、雙鴻、富世達最直接受惠。
全球液冷市場2030年達853億美元，二次側占約7成5 液冷各環節升級方向與單櫃價值量占比

結論
➢ 結論
AI算力需求推升資料中心擴建，電力與散熱成為建置瓶頸
2030年AI Workload容量上看156GW(CAGR 28.8%)；電網補強需3~5年，機房建置僅需1~2年。
接電時程落差推升現地供電需求，SOFC取得大型AIDC訂單
Oracle、Nebius、AEP/Google相繼採用BE燃料電池；2030年SOFC市場機會約22GW，資料中心占49%。
液冷由晶片延伸至整機與電源，規格與價值量同步升級
Rubin世代全面液冷，CDU放大至2.3~3MW；800VDC電源櫃液冷化，快接頭升級至大口徑與ZQD。
看好液冷冷板、快接頭與SOFC關鍵零組件供應鏈
奇鋐(3017)、富世達(6805)受惠液冷規格升級；高力(8996)切入SOFC供應鏈，宏致(3605)列入觀察。

Document 073: 2026年10月进出口数据解读：10月出口延续高增势头，油价上涨带动进口增速显著加快

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月进出口数据解读：10月出口延续高增势头，油价上涨带动进口增速显著加快.pdf
Report source: 东方金诚
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
10月出口延续高增势头，油价上涨带动进口增速显著加快
——2026年 10月进出口数据解读
研究发展部执行总监 冯琳
根据 11月 10日海关总署公布的数据，以美元计价，2026年10月出口额同比增长 35.2%，9月为增
长 36.3%，1-10月出口额累计同比增长 22.7%，2025年全年为 5.4%；10月进口额同比增长 41.6%，9月
为增长 31.0%，1-10月进口额同比增长29.0%，2025年全年为0.3%。
1、10月出口增速继续保持高位，主要原因是全球AI投资热潮对芯片等相关商品出口的拉动效应增
大，高新技术产品出口也在加快；当月出口增速较上月略有回落，主要是汽车及传统劳动密集型商品出
口增速有所放缓。
以美元计价，10月出口额同比增长 35.2%，增速较上月放缓 1.1个百分点，继续处于历史罕见高位，
主要受两个因素支撑：一是尽管下半年以来全球 AI估值出现两轮调整，但当前全球 AI投资热潮尚未减
弱，芯片等电子产品涨价效应还在进一步发酵，带动当月芯片出口增速高位上行。数据显示，10月芯片

（集成电路）出口数量同比下降2.5%，而出口金额同比增长137.1%，增速较上月加快0.3个百分点；10
月计算机等自动数据处理设备及其零部件出口额同比增长 114.8%，增速较上月加快 21.5个百分点。10
月这两类 AI直接相关商品出口额占整体出口额的比重升至 18.1%，对整体出口增速的拉动力达到 13.6
个百分点，较上月提高 0.6个百分点，是支撑年初以来出口高增的主要原因。二是近年国内制造业转型
升级取得进展，对包括计算机与通信技术产品、数控机床、医疗器械等高新技术产品出口的推动作用持
续显现。数据显示，10月高新技术产品出口额同比增长 66.6%，增速较上月加快 2.7个百分点，在整体
出口额中的占比达到 33.6%，较去年同期上升 6.3个百分点，显示出口商品结构持续改善正在成为推动
出口增长的一个重要因素。
10月出口增速较上月小幅下行 1.1个百分点，背后主要有两个原因：一是受上年同期基数大幅抬高
影响，当月汽车出口额同比增长37.6%，增速较上月回落24.3个百分点。不过，10月汽车出口量仍达到

110.8万辆，环比仅小幅下降7.2万辆，连续五个月保持在100万辆以上。二是10月受外需边际放缓等
影响，当月传统劳动密集型商品出口增速普遍放缓。数据显示，10月摩根大通全球制造业 PMI为 52.6%，
较上月下行 0.4个百分点，当月我国箱包、服装、玩具出口额同比增速分别为 17.9%、19.5%、27.5%，
较上月放缓 4.6、4.4、13.3个百分点。不过，受全球 AI投资热潮带动，近期外需仍处于偏强状态，对

### Page 2

2
我国整体出口有很强带动作用。
值得注意的是，在中欧经贸谈判背景下，10月我国对欧盟出口同比增长 24.3%，增速较上月回落 1
个百分点，继续处于高增长状态，背后或存在一定“抢出口”现象。当前我国与美国正在进行贸易战
“休战”延期谈判，美国对华关税水平较上年大幅下调，10月对美出口同比增长 32.9%，增速较上月回
落6.6个百分点，继续处于高增状态。
展望未来，受出口动能延续、上年同期基数抬高等因素的复合影响，11月出口增速有望保持在 26%
左右，强出口有望延续至年底。需要关注的是，7月以来全球 AI估值经历两轮大幅调整，美国费城半导
体指数月均值同比涨幅显著回落，由6月的157.3%降至10月的73.9%，截至11月9日进一步降至44.9%。
考虑到这一指标与我国芯片出口关联度较高，这意味着2027年我国芯片出口额增速将会高位下行，或对
整体出口增速带来较强的下拉效应。
2、10 月进口额同比高位加速，主要原因是国际油价上涨推动原油进口额同比增速明显加快，对进
口总额增速的拉动显著增强；同时，集成电路进口价格上涨带动其进口额增速加快，对进口总额增速的

拉动亦有所提升。此外，去年同期基数偏低、当月出口延续高增，也对进口高增形成一定支撑。
以美元计价，10月进口额同比增长41.6%，增速较上月大幅加快10.6个百分点。主要有以下原因：
一是 9月以来中东局势升级推高国际油价，10月原油进口均价同比上涨 62.6%（前值 31.5%），推动原
油进口额同比增长 41.0%，增速较上月大幅加快37.7个百分点，对进口总额增速的拉动由上月的0.3个
百分点提高至4.6个百分点。这意味着，当月进口总额增速加快的10.6个百分点中，约4.3个百分点可
由原油进口解释。
二是全球 AI投资热潮持续推动半导体价格，10月集成电路进口均价同比上涨78.3%（前值 71.6%），
带动集成电路进口额同比增速较上月加快 3.6个百分点至 78.3%；当月集成电路进口拉动进口总额增长
约 13.7个百分点，较上月提高 0.8个百分点。三是去年同期进口基数偏低，对10月进口同比表现有一
定支撑。四是我国对外贸易有“大出大进”特征，加工贸易占比较高，10月出口延续高增，必然带动进

口增速保持高位运行。
从其他主要进口商品来看，10月铁矿石进口价格延续小幅回落，同比下跌 6.7%（前值-1.9%），拖
累进口额同比降幅较上月扩大4.1个百分点至-11.5%；10月未锻轧铜及铜材进口价格同比涨幅因基数走
高而有所回落，但进口量同比回正、增长 3.0%（前值为下降 6.1%），带动当月进口额同比增速加快至
45.6%（前值 37.9%）；10月大豆进口均价同比上涨 12.9%（前值 11.4%），但因进口量同比降幅扩大至

### Page 3

3 
-22.5%（前值-7.7%），拖累当月进口额同比转负，下降12.5%（前值为增长2.8%）。 
展望后续，11月以来国际油价明显回落，原油进口价格上涨对进口总额增长的拉动作用预计将趋于
减弱；加之去年同期进口基数总体抬升，我们判断11月进口额同比增速将有所回落。不过，考虑到出口
仍将延续较快增长，集成电路进口将维持高增，加之稳增长政策发力会在一定程度上提振内需，预计11
月进口额同比将保持30%左右的高增水平。

上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和訊號 
經濟 
⚫ S&P Global 公布，9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數(PMI Composite Output 
Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4，創逾 5 年新高；服務業及製造業
PMI 分別升至 58.7 及 57.0。在內需強勁帶動下，新訂單與就業同步成
長，惟供應鏈瓶頸及能源成本攀升推升通膨壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國新屋銷售年化率為 68.4 萬戶，月增 6.4%、年減 2.0%，顯示銷
售較前月回升但需求尚未完全恢復。中西部與南部為主要支撐，惟房價
年減、庫存仍維持 48 萬戶以上，供給相對充裕，後續銷售能否延續仍
待觀察。 
⚫ 9 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.1，月減 7.0%、年減 12.7%，創近四個
月低點。當前狀況與預期指數同步下滑，反映高物價、燃料價格與貿易
不確定性持續壓抑信心；同時通膨預期升溫，後續消費動能仍待觀察。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 中國國家主席習近平於 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪美並與川普舉行峰會 ， 雙方
同意延長貿易休兵至 2027 年 1 月 10 日，推動部分商品降稅、建立 AI
對話機制及持續磋商稀土供應議題，並延續高層溝通。台灣議題未列入
成果清單，關稅、科技競爭及供應鏈等核心分歧仍未化解，後續談判發
展仍待觀察。 
短評 
⚫ 美國 9 月 PMI 強勁擴張，顯示企業活動與就業動能仍具韌性，但投入
成本與售價上升也反映通膨壓力升溫。相較之下，房市需求尚未全面恢
復，消費信心則明顯轉弱，顯示內需前景仍有隱憂；美中延長貿易休兵
雖有助穩定市場，但核心分歧未解，後續不確定性仍高。 
歐 
元 
區 
9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，服務業 PMI 增 1.4 點至 53，製造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7。 
短評 
⚫ 儘管 9 月經濟表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲
央行的鷹派態度，使得 10 月升息一碼的可能性些微增加，亦強化未來
升息預期。

日 
本 
9 月綜合 PMI 維持擴張，製造業表現仍具韌性 
經濟 ⚫ 9 月綜合 PMI 由 53.5 降至 52.5，製造業與服務業同步回落。 
短評 
⚫ 9 月製造業和服務業 PMI 同步降溫，但仍維持擴張；外需、AI 相關需
求與庫存回補支撐製造業，企業信心持續改善，而通膨壓力雖緩解但尚
未完全消退。 
中 
國 
美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
經濟 ⚫ 1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 習近平赴華府與美國總統川普舉行峰會，雙方同意貿易休戰期延長至
2027 年 1 月 10 日，並成立雙邊貿易理事會，但在科技管制、稀土供應
及關稅等核心議題上，仍未取得突破。 
⚫ 美中雙方就「300 億美元對 300 億美元」對等降稅架構達成共識，包
含中國製造的小型家電、玩具、節日裝飾品及兒童汽車安全座椅等，以
及美國生產的農產品、海鮮、原木、化妝品及醫療器材等。超過90%的
清單內產品將免除雙方此前相互加徵的關稅，並適用最惠國關稅待遇。 
短評

一、 美國—PMI 大幅走升、新屋銷售回穩，消費信心薄弱，美中關係暫現緩和
訊號 
標普全球 (S&P Global) 公布， 2026 年 9 月美國採購經理人綜合指數 (PMI 
Composite Output Index)初值由上月的 56.0 升至 58.4， 連續第四個月攀升 ， 創2021
年 7 月以來新高；其中服務業採購經理人指數(Services PMI)由 56.5 升至 58.7，創
59 個月高點，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 由 53.9 升至 57.0，創
2022 年 5 月以來最高水準，顯示美國企業活動持續強勁擴張。受國內市場需求穩
健帶動，製造業與服務業新訂單同步加速成長，推升整體產出增速升至逾 5 年來
最快水準，企業亦積極擴增人力，就業成長速度創 2022 年 6 月以來新高。另一
方面，積壓訂單增加及供應鏈延遲加劇，反映產能利用率持續攀升；加以近期油
價上漲推升燃料及運輸成本，使企業投入成本增幅升至 2022 年 10 月以來最高，
帶動售價漲幅回升，顯示通膨壓力有所升溫。儘管如此，企業對未來一年營運前
景仍維持正向看法，反映市場對美國經濟韌性及需求持續擴張仍具信心。 
2026 年 8 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整年化率為 68.4 萬戶 ， 較上月增加6.4%，
但較去年同期下降 2.0%，顯示新屋銷售較前月回升，惟仍低於去年同期水準。地
區別表現分歧，其中中西部及南部分別較上月增加 84.9%及 6.9%，東北部及西部
則分別減少 36.1%及 15.2%。價格方面，8 月新屋銷售中位價為 39.37 萬美元，較
上月小升 0.4%，但較去年同期下降 5.8%。供給方面，8 月底經季節性調整後待售
新屋數量為 48.3 萬戶，與上月持平，較去年同期減少 2.0%；按目前銷售速度估
算，供給月數為 8.5 個月，低於上月的 9.0 個月，與去年同期大致持平。整體而
言，8 月美國新屋銷售較前月回升，主要受中西部與南部銷售增加帶動，惟仍低
於去年同期，顯示需求動能尚未完全恢復。價格方面，中位價較去年同期下滑，
反映市場價格仍有調整壓力；同時待售新屋維持在 48 萬戶以上，供給月數仍達
8.5 個月，顯示整體供給相對充裕，後續仍須觀察銷售回升是否具持續性。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 9 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.1，低於上月
的 51.7， 月減7.0%、 年減12.7%， 為近四個月低點 ， 並較2026 年 1 月下降約 15%，
顯示消費者信心持續轉弱。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由51.9 降至 50.9，月減

1.9%、 年減15.7%； 消費者預期指數則由51.5 降至 46.3， 月減10.1%、 年減10.4%，
反映民眾對未來景氣與個人財務前景的看法明顯惡化。調查指出，消費者對高物
價的擔憂持續升高，當前與未來一年個人財務看法均較上月轉弱約一成；短期企
業展望也因燃料價格偏高與貿易爭端再度升溫而明顯下滑。政治立場方面，各陣
營受訪者普遍認為年初以來經濟前景轉弱，其中共和黨人信心較 1 月下降 20%，
民主黨人下降 13%。 通膨預期方面 ， 未來一年通膨預期由8 月的 4.0%升至 4.6%，
為今年 6 月以來最高，且高於今年 2 月的 3.4%(美伊爆發衝突之前)及 2024 年各
月水準；長期通膨預期亦由 3.3%升至 3.4%，結束連續三個月持平，並高於 2024
年 2.8%至 3.2%的區間。整體而言，9 月美國消費信心再度降溫，顯示高物價、燃
料價格與貿易不確定性持續壓抑民眾信心，後續仍須觀察通膨預期升溫是否進一
步影響消費動能。 
中國國家主席習近平於 2026 年 9 月 23 日至 25 日訪問美國，並於 9 月 24 日
在華府白宮與美國總統川普舉行峰會。會後，美中雙方未發布聯合聲明，而是分
別公布成果清單，顯示雙方在穩定雙邊關係及維持高層溝通方面取得部分共識，
包括同意建構「基於尊重、公平、對等的建設性戰略穩定關係」、推動 300 億美
元非敏感商品對等降稅安排、建立人工智慧 (AI)或「超級智慧」對話與風險溝通
機制，以及持續就稀土與關鍵礦產供應鏈議題進行磋商。此外，美方並表示中國
將於 2027 年及 2028 年分別進口至少 1,000 萬公噸美國煤炭。值得注意的是，台
灣議題並未出現在雙方公布的成果文件中；另一方面，中方則額外強調兩軍危機
溝通機制及合作搜尋失蹤美軍遺骸等事項。整體而言，本次川習會有助延續美中
對話與降低短期緊張情勢，惟雙方在關稅、科技競爭、供應鏈安全及其他戰略議
題上的結構性分歧仍未完全化解，後續談判進展及雙邊互動方向仍有待持續觀察。 
二、歐元區—9 月景氣增強，進一步推高升息預期 
 歐元區 9 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)增 1.1 點至 53.1，且為連續四個月上
升。其中服務業PMI 增 1.4 點至 53，創近 10 個月高點，主要反映 AI 與國防投資
活動增加，帶動商業服務需求，抵銷能源價格上漲對消費購買力的負面影響。製
造業 PMI 則持平於 52.7，不過，德國萊茵河水位下降，沿岸較為發達的化工、製
藥業物流成本增加，因而暫時壓抑製造業上行空間。值得注意的是，由於國際原
油價格回升，綜合投入、產出物價指數皆上升至近 4 個月最高。故儘管 9 月經濟
表現優於預期，但通膨壓力也同步升溫，再加上先前歐洲央行的鷹派態度，使得

三、日本—9 月綜合 PMI 維持擴張，製造業表現仍具韌性 
 日本 9 月綜合 PMI 由 53.5 降至 52.5，製造業與服務業同步回落，經濟成長動
能較前兩個月的高檔有所降溫。製造業 PMI 下跌 0.8 點至 54.1，雖然產出、新訂
單及出口訂單增速放緩，但外部需求仍相當強勁，尤其 AI 相關需求可能持續帶
動出口表現，加上企業開始重建庫存、供應鏈瓶頸尚未完全解除，預料補庫存需
求將在未來數月繼續支撐生產成長。服務業 PMI 下降 0.9 點至 51.6，受到工資、
運輸及能源成本上升影響 ， 需求動能略有降溫 ， 但積壓訂單與出口業務有所改善 。
另，投入成本與售價漲幅均較前期放緩，顯示通膨壓力正逐步緩解，但整體物價
水準仍維持高檔。此外，企業對未來景氣的信心持續改善，未來活動指數升至七
個月高點，反映企業普遍預期經濟擴張趨勢仍可望延續。 
四、中國—美中貿易延長休戰，惟後續協商成效仍待觀察 
1-8 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 6.6%、17.6%，其中營業收入年
增率較上期(1-7 月)略增 0.1 個百分點，但利潤年增率下降 1.9 個百分點。受惠於
金屬、塑化原物料上漲，加上 AI 需求擴張，以及政府推動高科技產業發展等因
素，營收成長貢獻度較高者包含有色金屬加工(營收、利潤年增 31.1%、71.8%)、
計算機、通信和其他電子設備製造業(營收、利潤年增20.1%、109.9%)、化學原料
及製品(營收、利潤年增 9.1%、51%)。此外，電氣機械及器材業、汽車業營收年
增率雖分別為 11.5%、2.9%，但獲利分別年減 5.2%、16%。非金屬礦物製品業營
收、獲利下滑 9.5、46.7%，黑色金屬加工業營收、獲利分別年減 1.1%、62.4%，
反映產業持續進行減產所致。整體而言，部分產業受惠於短期原物料價格波動，
以及受到政策推動的高科技產業表現較佳，惟部分產業因陷入產能調整週期，或
政府減少支持等因素而表現較弱，產業分化明顯。 
展望未來，儘管美中雙方互相降低等值 300 億美元產品關稅，惟考量雙方每
年貿易總額逾 4,000 億美元，且降稅產品並非半導體、先進製程設備、電動車、
軍民兩用技術等關鍵領域，因此實質效益有限。另外，雖然美中雙方同意延長貿
易休戰，但休戰期只延長兩個月，反映美國仍保留談判槓桿，持續給予中國稀土
供應履約的壓力。後續將關注美中能否在休戰期間就開放市場、科技競爭、供應

五、印度及東南亞—亞銀上修亞洲及太平洋經濟體 2026 年 GDP 預估至 4.7% 
亞 洲 開 發 銀 行 (ADB) 最 新 經 濟 展 望 (Asian Development Outlook, ADO 
September 2026 )上修 2026 年亞洲及太平洋地區經濟體 2026 年經濟成長率(GDP)
至 4.7%(7 月預估 4.5%)、2027 年為 4.5%。ADB 認為，亞洲開發中經濟體雖面臨
中東衝突延長、俄烏戰爭再升溫、能源價格高檔震盪及強烈聖嬰現象等逆風，但
在政府刺激政策、民間投資及 AI 帶動科技出口支撐下，區域經濟仍展現韌性。
通膨方面，2026 年通膨率下修至 3.9%，惟能源與糧食價格壓力仍高，2027 年通
膨預測上修至 3.3%。 
區域經濟表現方面 ， 南亞成長最強勁 ，2026 年 GDP 成長率預估上修至 6.4%，
主要受印度內需與投資帶動；東亞則受中國內需疲弱影響，成長維持平穩；東協
國家(ASEAN)上調至 4.7%， 其中越南 、 馬來西亞及泰國受惠於科技與製造業投資
而獲上修。中亞及西亞、太平洋地區則面臨能源及食品價格上漲壓力，成長預測
略為下修至 3.7%、3.0%。(詳下表) 
ADB 認為未來最大風險包括地緣政治衝突升級、聖嬰 現象造成糧食供應衝
擊，以及 AI 相關資產可能出現價格重估。整體而言，AI 驅動的科技投資仍是亞
洲經濟體最重要的成長動能來源，而能源、糧食及氣候風險將成為影響各國通膨
與信用風險的重要變數。

五、台灣—AI 浪潮推升接單動能，外銷訂單再創新高 
8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，首度突破千億美元大關，其中受惠於 ASIC
晶片需求帶動，資通產品接單翻倍、電子產品接單亦大增 83.9%，惟光學器材因
面板需求維持低檔，年減 5%。非科技產品方面，基本金屬年增 26.8%，係因銅箔
及銅箔基板受惠 AI 相關供應鏈需求及銅價續漲，加以鍍鋅鋼需求回溫；機械年
增 28.3%，係因半導體業者增加資本支出，帶動半導體設備、自動化設備需求延
續；化學品年增 15.4%，主因藥品接單成長，加上產業用化學品需求持續增加；
塑橡膠製品年增 6.7%， 主係塑膠原料受原料上漲而推升產品售價 ， 加上部分塑膠
製品訂單回溫。展望未來，經濟部統計處預估 2026 年外銷訂單有望突破 1 兆美
元，在 AI 相關需求持續擴張帶動下，科技產品表現可望維持強勁，而傳統產業
中下游產品亦可望受惠於相關供應鏈需求，惟上游原物料價格競爭激烈，後續仍
須關注第四季消費旺季是否有相關備貨需求。

1 
2308 台達電(Delta)

報告日期： 2026/11/02 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 營收 1970.6 億 ， 毛利率34.1%， 營利率16.6%，EPS 9.04
元。受到供應鏈缺料以及拉貨遞延影響， 3Q26 毛利率不如預
期。  
 預估 4Q26 營收將維持成長趨勢，毛利率部分，隨著隨著庫存
預計開始去化，以及部分產品漲價部分產品為反映原物料成本
而調漲價格，毛利率將有望回升。  
 HVDC 部分， ±400V 已在下半年開始出貨， 800V 系統亦持續
發展推進，同時液冷、電源機櫃、 DC-DC 模組等新產品線持續
放量；公司亦持續推進固態氧化物燃料電池 (SOFC) ，目前已有
海外大型客戶接觸送樣，初期產能逾 100MW ，預計 2027 年正
式量產。

一、受缺料影響， 3Q26 毛利率 34.1% ，EPS 9.04 元 
台達電 3Q26 營收 1970.6 億， QoQ +7.5% ，YoY +31.1% ，略低
於公司預期，主要由於供應鏈缺料以及資料中心建置延遲，導致
部分貨品出貨遞延 。 就產品組合來看 ，3Q26 電源及零部件 (Power 
Electronics) 佔整體營收的 54% ，交通 (Mobility) 佔 5% ，自動化
(Automation) 佔 8% 基礎建設 (Infrastructure) 佔 33% 。而獲利方
面， 3Q26 營業毛利 672 億， QoQ +2.8% ，YoY +28.1% ，毛利率
34.1% ，QoQ -1.6ppts ，YoY -0.8ppts ，公司表示，第三季存貨增
加，認列約 39 億元的備抵存貨跌價損失。主要由於現有產品缺
料，以及新產品客戶新平台量產較慢、拉貨延後所致，預計第四
季至明年第一季陸續消化並回沖。 3Q26 營業淨利 326.2 億，營利
率 16.6% ，業外淨利 10.46 億，稅後淨利 234.95 億，單季稅後
EPS 9.04 元。

二、庫存去化、部分產品漲價， 4Q26 營運有望回升  
展望 4Q26 ，公司表示，第四季營收確定將維持成長趨勢，而毛利
率部分，公司亦預估，第四季毛利率將優於第三季，主要由於隨
著庫存預計從第四季開始去化，並在明年第一季陸續消化完畢，
而存貨順利出貨後即可回沖。另外，第四季部分產品為反映原物
料成本而調漲價格 。 預估4Q26 營收 2181 億，QoQ +10.7% ，YoY 
+35.0% ，毛利率 35.5% 。營利率 18.2% ，稅後淨利 283 億，單季
稅後 EPS 10.89 元。

投資評等：買進 
目標價位：1680 元 
     27x-2027 EPS 
參考價位：1335 元 
     21.3 X 2027 EPS 
     2026/10/30 收盤

三. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 37.53/62.49 元 
展望 2026 年，公司表示， 2026 年全年整體資料中心相關營收佔
比將接近 5 成，其中， AI 電源解決方案營收佔比有望達 25% ，液
冷散熱則大約佔 13% 。預估 2026 全年營收 7574 億元， YoY 
+36.5% ，毛利率 35.5% ，營利率 17.3% ，稅後淨利 974.8 億，全
年稅後 EPS 37.53 元。展望 2027 年，受惠於 CSP 持續擴大資本
支出， AI 資料中心建置需求延續，公司預期， AI 相關營收與液冷
系統產品 2027 年的營收佔比將進一步攀升。 HVDC 部分， ±400V
已在下半年開始出貨 ， 由NVIDIA 推動的 800V 系統亦持續發展推
進，同時液冷、電源機櫃、 DC-DC 模組等新產品線持續放量，此
外，公司亦持續推進固態變壓器 (SST) 與固態氧化物燃料電池
(SOFC) ，初期產能逾 100MW ，預計 2027 年正式量產，目前已有
海外大型客戶接觸送樣。而產能部分，公司亦宣布，將於馬來西
亞檳城設立大型生產據點，預計五年內產能追上泰國廠區。另外，
針對 Vicor 提出侵權訴訟以及美國 ITC 啟動調查，公司則表示，
公司自身擁有多項 IP，且 ITC 程序冗長，短期內對公司出貨與客
戶不會造成影響。預估 2027 全年營收 1 兆 695 億元， YoY 
+41.2% ，毛利率 36.5% ，營利率 20.0% ，稅後淨利 1623 億，全
年稅後 EPS 62.49 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 1680 元 
考量到 CSP 持續擴大資本支出， AI 資料中心建置需求延續，公司
具備關鍵產業地位，且漲價效益及遞延訂單後續將陸續反映，將
有望持續挹注公司營收及獲利成長動能。以公司 2027 年 EPS 來
看，目前股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 21~22 倍本益比，評價仍具
上修空間，給於買進投資建議，目標價 1680 元(27x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
台達電 (Delta) 成立於 1971 年，以製造繞線式磁性元件起家，早期
專注於筆電 (NB) 、桌機 (DT) 、螢幕 (Monitor) 、伺服器 (Server) 、印
表機 (Printer) 及網通等 IT 相關產品之電源供應器與散熱風扇，憑
藉深厚的核心技術延伸發展，穩居全球磁性與散熱元件重要供應
商及全球最大交換式電源供應器龍頭。因應全球電子產業轉變與
環保節能意識抬頭，台達電近年積極跨足工業自動化、智慧安防
及綠能領域，逐步從關鍵零組件製造商轉型為「整體節能解決方
案提供者」，成為全球電源管理與散熱解決方案的專業領導廠商。
目前集團深耕電源及零組件、自動化與基礎設施等核心業務，並
進一步拓展至交通領域，依 2025 年產品結構比重來看，分別為電

客戶；主要競爭同業在國內包括光寶科、全漢、美磊、康舒等廠
商，國外市場則有 Panasonic 、Vishay 等國際大廠。

資料來源：台達電

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

2. 液冷散熱成高階 AI 基礎設施標配，估 2026 年滲透率達 53% 
根據 TrendForce 最新研究顯示，隨著 AI 基礎設施建置浪潮及高
階晶片運算功率不斷攀升，單晶片熱設計功耗已超越 1kW ，使液

4 
冷散熱技術正式成為高階 AI 基礎設施的標準配置。在 NVIDIA 、
AMD 與 Google 等巨頭的高階晶片與整櫃式方案強力推動下，預
估 2026 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片的滲透率將從 2025 年的 33% 大幅
躍升至 53%，並在 2027 年進一步逼近 60%。其中， Google 因積
極導入自研 TPU 並率先全面推廣整櫃式液冷設計，成為主要推手
之一。此外，隨著 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 平台全面導入全液冷架構，
水冷板、分歧管及冷卻分配系統等關鍵零組件需求隨之大增，帶
動相關供應鏈如 AVC、Cooler Master 等業者積極搶攻各大 CSP
與 AI 平台商機。

圖三、 2023-2027 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片滲透率預估

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

3. 預估 2026 年全球資料中心用電需求年增 31% 
根據  TrendForce 產業研究顯示 ， 受惠於AI 應用需求激增與雲端
服務供應商 (CSP) 加速擴建基礎設施 ， 全球資料中心電力需求容量
預計將由 2025 年的 122.9GW 攀升至 2026 年的 161GW ，YoY 
+31% ，並於 2027 年維持超過 30% 的高成長率；其中， AI 伺服器
為核心增長動能，占整體電力需求比重由 2025 年的 25% 提升至
2026 年的 33.4% ，預計 2027 年更將突破 40% ，同步帶動散熱與
供電等非 IT 設備需求，促使資料中心規劃導入高壓直流供電
(HVDC) 以優化供配電效率。然而，電網供電增速卻未能跟上需求
擴張 ， 全球資料中心電力供需自2026 年起開始背離 ， 並將於2028
年後因電網併網與輸配電建設交付遞延而顯著擴大；預估至 2030
年，全球資料中心電力需求將達 490.7GW ，但電網可供容量僅
222.6GW ，供需缺口高達 268GW( 其中資料中心部署量最大的美
國自 2028 年起遞延加劇，至 2030 年缺口預計超過 170GW) ，未
來此一實質短缺與帳面落差能否透過業者自建發電及電網改善來
填補，仍有待觀察。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 庫存去化、部分產品漲價， 4Q26 營運有望回升  
展望 4Q26 ，公司表示，第四季營收確定將維持成長趨勢，而毛利
率部分，公司亦預估，第四季毛利率將優於第三季，主要由於隨
著庫存預計從第四季開始去化，並在明年第一季陸續消化完畢，
而存貨順利出貨後即可回沖。另外，第四季部分產品為反映原物
料成本而調漲價格 。 預估4Q26 營收 2181 億，QoQ +10.7% ，YoY 
+35.0% ，毛利率 35.5% 。營利率 18.2% ，稅後淨利 283 億，單季
稅後 EPS 10.89 元。

2344 華邦電(Winbond)

報告日期： 2026/11/06  研 究 員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要  
 3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 為 8.06 元。 受惠記憶體
報價上漲與產品組合改善，第三季毛利率達 70.57% ，預估第
四季營收 839.78 億元， QoQ +2.0% ，毛利率 70.77% 。 
 擴產與 16 奈米轉進帶動 2027 年位元出貨成長。  高雄廠
Module A 新增產能及台中廠 Flash 擴產陸續貢獻，搭配
DDR4 、LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 需求，支持營運成長。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 23.29 ／37.45 元。 AI 伺服器與
企業級 SSD 需求為主要動能， CUBE 及矽電容拓展新應用。

一、3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 營收持續季增 
華邦電為利基型記憶體廠，產品涵蓋客製化記憶體、NOR Flash 及
SLC NAND。3Q26 營收 823.31 億元，QoQ +37.6%、YoY +278.2%；
營業毛利 581.01 億元，毛利率 70.57%；營業利益 437.84 億元，營
業利益率 53.18%；稅後純益 354.22 億元， EPS 7.58 元。營收結構
以客製化記憶體占 58%、Flash 占 32%、邏輯 IC 占 9%。其中，客
製化記憶體平均售價季增近 60%，Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，為
第三季成長主因。展望第四季，公司表示 AI 需求維持強勁，部分
PC 與消費性客戶調整拉貨，整體營運仍將成長，惟增幅低於前幾
季。考量合約價格續升及產品組合改善，預估 4Q26 營收 839.78 億
元，QoQ +2.0%；毛利率 70.77%，營業利益 448.28 億元，稅後 EPS 
8.06 元。

二、高雄廠擴產，支應 DDR4 及 LPDDR4 長期需求 
公司持續供應 DDR3、DDR4 及 LPDDR4，受惠主要記憶體原廠調
整產品配置，以及車用、工業客戶產品生命週期長、替換供應來
源不易，長期供貨需求具支撐。高雄廠 Module A 月產能預計由約
1.5 萬片提高至 2.4 萬至 2.5 萬片，新增產能於 2027 年陸續貢獻，
並提高 16 奈米製程比重，同時增加晶圓投入與單片晶圓位元產
出 。 法人看好擴產搭配製程轉進可帶動 2027 年 DRAM 位元出貨顯
著成長，中信預估增幅超過 100%。本報告採新增產能與製程效益
逐步反映的假設，預估 2027 年營收逐季成長。長期方面，董事會
通過 731 億元資本支出，用於高雄廠 Module B 土建、機電及長交
期設備，規劃月產能約 5 萬至 6 萬片，預計 2029 年下半年開始小
量出貨，並以長期供貨協議支應策略客戶需求。 
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：180 元 
      5 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：127.5 元 
     3.4 X 2027 EPS

2

三、AI 帶動 CUBE 與矽電容拓展新應用 
AI 伺服器機櫃內 GPU 、 BMC 、 SmartNIC 及交換器等元件均需 Flash
儲存韌體與設定資料，隨運算及管理架構複雜化，帶動使用顆數
與容量提升。公司預估供應 AI 生態系的 Flash 位元出貨量於 2026
年成長 112%，2027 年再增 67%，其中高容量 NOR Flash 為主要動
能；企業級 SSD 亦增加 DRAM 需求，擴大公司在伺服器供應鏈的
參與 。 法人看好台中廠擴產與高容量產品組合改善 。 新產品方面，
CUBE 主要布局 AI 加速器與推論應用 ， 預計 2027 年下半年增加出
貨；矽電容已小量出貨，既有產能多獲客戶預訂，並透過代工模
式切入 AI 晶片、先進封裝及高速光模組應用，逐步增加產品附加
價值與長期營收來源。  
四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 180 元  
華邦電為全球最大 NOR Flash 廠亦是利基型 DRAM 廠，AI 帶動各
式記憶體需求增加，產能仍供不應求下，獲利將逐季成長。公司
針對邊緣 AI 發展之 CUBE 解決方案將於 2027 年開始貢獻 ， 矽電容
亦為新的業務機會，中長期而言 AI、HPC、IoT、自駕車等新產品
新應用將帶動更多記憶體需求。考量到 1) AI 帶動之記憶體供不應
求將延續至 2028 年、2)華邦電今明年營收獲利創高、3)CUBE、矽
電容具成長潛力，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 5 倍本益比，目標價 180
元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華邦電子於 1987 年九月由華新麗華集團轉投資成立，為自有品牌
IC 設計及製造廠商，並為全世界第五大 DRAM 供應商，且為國內
少數擁有晶圓廠之積體電路設計公司，同時也是全球利基型記憶
體的主要供應商。2008 年公司將邏輯 IC 事業分割成立新唐科技，
新唐產品線以消費性電子 IC 、電腦 IC 兩大產品線為主，並同時擁
有一座 6 吋晶圓廠。華邦電在 NOR Flash 為全球最大供應商，產
品上從中低容量市場拓展至中高容量市場。NOR Flash 主要同業為
旺宏、兆易創新、Infineon。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望

1. 2026 下半年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 2026 年 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 下半年，預估記憶體求維持強勁下，價格持續上漲，唯
因價格基期已高，加上傳統消費性產品追價力道有限，漲幅不若
上半年，不過預估今年隨著價格上漲，加上製程轉換帶動位元出

公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
貨量增加，及新產能貢獻，營收逐季成長，毛利率持續提升，預
估華邦電 2026 年營收 2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%) ， 毛利率 67.17% ，
稅後淨利 1,047.92 億元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

3. 2027 年記憶體更加緊缺，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之記憶體缺貨狀況延續，加上全球新增產能
有限，緊缺情況更加嚴峻，而儘管終端電子產品需求不一，不過
未來 AI 落地後每單位產品之記憶體搭載量持續成長，使得記憶體
位元出貨提升 ，2027 年公司營運仍正向 。DDR4 在全球三大廠逐步
退出下價格自 2Q25 起漲，且因終端電子產品仍有一定需求，價格
將維持高檔，NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 亦因供給端增產有限，價格
逐步回升，其中 SLC Nand 因大廠退出，華邦將成為全球最大供應
商，而應用於 AI Server 的高容量 NOR Flash 持續供不應求，預期
將推升價格上行至 2027 年。華邦電製程技術方面，20 奈米 DRAM
產品仍為主要出貨主力，應用於 DDR3、DDR4、LPDDR 產品，16
奈米製程已開發完成，2027 年將有一定比例轉換至 16 奈米；NOR 
Flash 營收穩定成長 ， NAND Flash 24 奈米及 32 奈米 SLC Nand 今年
2Q 貢獻顯著提升。 因應記憶體需求強勁， 公司 Flash 與 DRAM 擴
產計畫進行中，華邦電 2025 年資本支出為台幣 55 億元，2026 年
將提升至 395 億元，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%，大部分用
於高雄產能及製程提升，少部分用於台中廠製程提升，高雄廠產
能將由現在 15k 擴充至年底的 24k，屆時位元出貨量可望翻倍，同
時啟動高雄 Module B 投資計劃，規劃月產能可達 50k~ 60k，將分
階段擴充，預估 2027 年 1 月動工，2029 年 1 月裝機，2029 年底量
產。2027 年預估 DRAM 、NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 價格持續上漲帶
動營收獲利成長，預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛
利率 72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

4. CUBE 及矽電容為新成長動能 
AI 時代來臨，華邦電長期專注於利基型 DRAM ，針對 AI 將主攻邊
緣運算市場，邊緣運算之記憶體容量不需像 AI Server 那麼高，為
華邦電可著墨之區塊，因此華邦電提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra 
Bandwidth Element 客製化超高頻寬記憶體元件)解決方案，CUBE
具備超低功耗和超高性能、小尺寸、高成本效益，從而大幅提升
系統能力、回應時間和能源效率，以滿足邊緣運算平臺上快速增
長的 AI 應用需求。CUBE 優點在於 1)SoC 推疊在上面，散熱效果
較好、2) 可在 CUBE Die 上做穿孔(TSV)，降低成本、3) 3D 推疊
適合較小的終端裝置。目前華邦電與多家客戶開發 CUBE 方案，
預估 2026 年底開始貢獻，2027 年放量。另外因應 AI 晶片功耗劇
增、瞬間電流波動，以及先進封裝的空間限制，矽電容具有極低
的等效串聯電感與寄生電感，能在極短時間內補償電流，維持電

源完整性，避免電壓驟降，也彌補傳統 MLCC 的不足，華邦電因

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 毛利率升至 70.57%，EPS 7.58 元 
華邦電 3Q26 營收 823.31 億元(QoQ+37.6%、YoY+278.2%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 742.84 億元(QoQ+43.7%)。受惠客製化記憶體平均售
價季增近 60%、Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，合併毛利率提升至
70.57% ， 營業利益 437.84 億元 ， 歸屬母公司稅後淨利 354.22 億元，
EPS 7.58 元。展望第四季，記憶體合約價格持續上升，惟部分 PC
與消費性客戶調整拉貨，預估營收 839.78 億元(QoQ+2.0%)，毛利
率 70.77%，EPS 8.06 元。

二. 2026 年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 年下半年，AI 伺服器需求強勁，帶動 DDR4、LPDDR4
及高容量 Flash 價格持續上漲，惟第四季部分 PC 與消費性客戶調
整拉貨，營運成長幅度較第三季收斂。預估華邦電 2026 年營收
2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%)，毛利率 67.17%，稅後淨利 1,047.92 億
元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

三. 2027 年擴產與製程轉進帶動成長，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 伺服器與企業級 SSD 需求持續成長，DDR4、
LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 供需維持緊俏，加上高雄廠新增產能
與 16 奈米製程轉進，帶動位元出貨增加，CUBE 及矽電容亦逐步
貢獻營收。預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛利率
72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

### Page 2

New capacity in expansion: The company continues to expand its production scale to 
fulfill rising clients’ orders from LEO satellite and innovative business. We expect to see 
growing LEO satellite revenues contribution from 75% in 2025 to 97% in 2028E. 
Management notes the company has started the planning of next-stage expansion, and 
will start to expand capacity in Taiwan, Vietnam and US sites from 4Q26E and onwards 
to diversify the global production sites, improve the product delivery capabilities, and 
supply elasticity.

Earnings revision: We factor in UMT 3Q26 earnings and revise down earnings by 5%/ 
10%/ 9% in 2026-28E mainly on lower GM in 2026-28E by 3.8ppts~3.9ppts, reflecting 
longer time of transition to new LEO satellites platform, while we expect to see 
continuous GM improvement ahead on rising LEO satellite buisness carrying higher GM.

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(telecom operators who face competition from LEO satellite operators). Our target 
2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 35.1x (vs. previously at 37.1x) which is based on 
peers’ avg. PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM, due to lower avg. OPM in 
2029-30E at 38% (vs. previously at 42%). We apply the 35.1x multiple to 2029E EPS and

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 35.1x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,188. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Nan Ya PCB  | Asia Pacific
In-line 3Q26 gross margin
3Q26 results:
• 3Q revenue was NT$16.4bn (+21% q/q, +49% y/y).
• GM reached 29.3% (+460bps q/q, +1,970bps y/y), slightly above our estimate
but in line with the Street, driven by higher ABF (low 30s  %) and BT GM
(high 20s  %).  PCB remains  loss-making on the gross margin level.
• OP was NT$4.3bn (+49% q/q, +597% y/y), 1% above our estimate and the
Street, for OpM of 26.4% (+500bps q/q, +2,070bps y/y).

• However, non-op income was below our estimate and the Street. Tax rate of
~26% was again higher than our estimate at 16% and the Street at 19%.
• Thus, NP came in at NT$3.3bn (+46% q/q, +355% y/y), 13% below our
estimate/8% below the Street, for EPS of  NT$5.10.

Key Takeaways
NYPCB's 3Q GM is  largely in line with both buy-side and sell-side estimates.
The 500bps sequential improvement in OpM looks positive to us. Similar to

Unimicron's 3Q results, we view NYPCB's slight miss as an expectation
mismatch...
...rather than reflecting any structural concerns.
We also expect NYPCB to gain share for ABF substrates for AMD's server CPUs,
as one Japanese competitor reallocates more capacity to another CPU customer.
We remain constructive on all ABF substrate stocks in our coverage.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Phison Electronics Corp  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 gross margin miss; our
conservative stance on Nand
modules playing out
Looking back to our February downgrade note: In AI boosts NAND demand, but
Asian module maker margins may soon compress, we noted that Phison's gross
margin could decline in 2Q26 or 3Q26, once it ran out of low-cost inventory.  While
Phison's low-cost inventory cushioned the consumer Nand module margin downside,
its overall NAND module margin started to miss in 3Q26, which means the AI

storage part of its business (eSSD, and cSSD for AI PC, together 40% of revenue)
may see margin decline.
Please see Exhibit 1  for our earnings review. The company's 3Q26 gross margin
declined to 59%, below our 63.5% and the street's 60.3%, also missing the
company's previous commentary about a sustainable gross margin in 3Q26 given its
low cost inventory. The company hopes its AiDaptive module and service business
can pick up and offset the margin pressure in the conventional module business in

the coming quarters.
Exhibit 1: 2Q Earnings Review

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Phison Electronics Corp (8299.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-term
growth rate of 9.0%, a terminal growth rate of 4.0%, and a dividend payout ratio assumption
of 51.2%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected eMMC controller IC outsourcing rate
n Faster SSD market growth
n Market share gain in ultrabooks and SSDs
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than expected NAND demand

n Milder impact on global NAND supply
n Non-acceptance of price hikes on NAND modules

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo on Vanguard International (VIS), 
we raise our forecasts and 12M TP and upgrade our rating one notch to 
Buy (1), given a widened disconnect between strengthened fundamentals 
yet weakened valuations. Assessed in our Asia Foundry report, VIS’s wafer 
loadings should approach full through 2027, thanks to demand spillover 
from AI scaling augmented by TSMC’s fab optimisation, with better ASP 
adding to growth. While the equity markets appear to be haunted by the US 
mid-term elections, management sees it as a “healthy wakeup call” to 
anyone with overheated AI expectations; we concur.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. Since operating metrics at VIS 
look broadly intact except for higher ASP as some customers’ previous 
long-term agreements expired later, we only finetune our forecasts by 
increasing our 2026-28E EPS 2-6%. We stick with our view that VIS will 
enjoy counter-seasonal prints through 2027 with sustained strong earnings 
growth, thanks again to a paradigm shift we’ve observed in the specialty 
foundry market benefiting VIS, aided by its fast 12” capacity ramp.

A healthy wake-up call? We were impressed by management’s 
commentary about potential impact from the US mid-term elections that it 
should cool down any overheated expectations for the AI buildouts in the 
markets, since deployments among AI factories, power infrastructure, 
environment and social welfare need to be reviewed and optimised for a 
right balance. That said, management views this as a healthy wake-up call 
to the markets since AI has walked in our daily lives, becoming a 
fundamental demand for an unreversed secular trend. We believe its 
commentary supports our view calling for likely a soft-landing 2028 when 
hyperscalers’ investments slow down and new supply catches up to help 
discover inflated demand, fulfil unmet demand and manage excess 
inventory for a healthy growth and balance between AI and non-AI 
consumer ecosystems. Though the correction could be painful, the equity 
markets appear to have discounted this risk now than later, which looks to 
set a healthy wakeup call to the equity market as well, in our view.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we trim our 12M TP 
for VIS to TWD200 (previous: TWD210), on our lower ROE-adjusted target 
PBR of 5x (previous: 5.2x) pegging to our lowered target multiple for TSMC 
(2330 TT, TWD2,235, Buy [1]) That said, we upgrade our rating one notch 
to Buy (1) on valuation grounds with key downside risks of the current chip 
upturn ending earlier and 12” ramp/execution coming in below expectation.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 3-11% above the consensus 
forecasts likely due to our higher trajectory of ASP assumptions.

9 November 2026

Upgrading: a healthy reset in expectations

➢ Fine-tuning up our forecasts after the results call 
➢ A healthy wakeup call from the US mid-term elections? 
➢ Upgrading rating to Buy (1) with a revised 12M TP of TWD200

Company profile 
Founded in December 1994 with stocks listed in March 1998, Vanguard International Semiconductor 
Corp. (VIS) successfully transformed its business model from memory to complementary metal oxide 
semiconductor (CMOS) logic foundry focusing on specialty technologies such as mixed signal, power 
analog and embedded memory, growing as one leading specialty foundry in the world. Leveraging a 
strategic relationship with its major shareholder TSMC, VIS operates wafer fabs in Taiwan and 
Singapore, servicing a high variety of customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs around the 
world.

### Page 5

5

Vanguard International Semiconductor (5347 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 9 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
VIS's board of directors (BoD) consists of 9 members, including 5 independent directors,
representing 56% of the total board seats. The board comprises members with diverse
backgrounds in business management, finance, industry, and academia, providing a broad
range of expertise and perspectives.
We believe the majority-independent board structure and the directors’ collective experience in
leadership, corporate governance, industry expertise, financial management, risk management,
and sustainability strengthen the effectiveness of the BoD and support VIS's long-term growth
and corporate governance practices.
Capital management 1 VIS pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
Related party &
transaction 1 Sales to related parties were around 1-2% of its total revenue each year. The percentage is insignificant and thus we see limited risk for VIS.
S Supply chain management 1
VIS has established a comprehensive sustainable supply chain management framework to
ensure suppliers comply with its sustainability requirements covering environmental protection,
labor and human rights, occupational health and safety, business ethics, and responsible
sourcing. The company requires suppliers to adhere to its "VIS Supplier Code of Conduct" and
"Corporate Sustainability Policy", and conducts supplier sustainability assessments and audits
as part of its supplier management process. We believe these efforts strengthen VIS's supply
chain resilience and help mitigate supply chain risks.
E Water & wastewater management 2
VIS is committed to improving water efficiency and sustainable water management. In 2022, the
company implemented the ISO 46001 Water Efficiency Management System to analyze water
consumption patterns, identify significant water-use equipment, evaluate water-saving
opportunities, and establish improvement measures. VIS also provides annual water
management training at its Taiwan facilities to promote continuous improvement in water-use
efficiency. We believe these efforts demonstrate the company's commitment to responsible
water stewardship and help reduce operational risks associated with water resources.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
VIS adopts a comprehensive resource management approach to waste management and has
established waste management procedures in accordance with ISO 14001 standards. Through
dedicated waste management personnel and regular employee training, the company promotes
effective waste reduction, classification, collection, storage, and treatment practices across its
operations.
E GHG emissions 1
VIS adopts a source-segregation and multi-stage treatment approach, supported by high-
efficiency air pollution control systems, to effectively manage VOC and acidic/alkaline gas
emissions from semiconductor manufacturing processes.
The company's emission levels remain well below regulatory limits, reflecting its commitment to
environmental compliance and sustainable operations.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

두산 
에너빌리티 
(034020)

시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 
SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수
주 확대 본격화 
허민호

minho.hur@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 85,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
56,700 
유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

Date: 未知日期

### Page 1

⾼盛交易台表⽰，当下这波抛售更像 1998 年流动性危机，⽽⾮ 2000 年泡沫破裂，是杠杆强
制出清⽽⾮商业逻辑被证伪。市场剧烈重定价的是建设融资与时间节奏，⽽⾮底层需求。
此外，市场⽬前最关注的电⼒与许可问题恰恰是最不重要的⼀条，供给受限并不等于需求
消失。真正需要回答的问题是：收⼊端能否最终证明所投⼊资本的合理性。
摘要：
科技股遭遇剧烈抛售，但究竟是杠杆出清还是商业模式被证伪，这决定了截然相反的操作⽅
向。
据追⻛交易台，⾼盛 FICC 与股票交易台在 11 ⽉ 6 ⽇报告中表⽰，纳斯达克指数单⽇下跌 5.5% ，
费城半导体指数重挫 12% ，标普 500 指数下跌 4.4% 。⾼盛现⾦股票业务数据显⽰，当⽇美国现
⾦股票业务净卖出 30 亿美元，其中信息技术与通信服务板块占 22 亿美元，科技板块累计净卖出
规模已扩⼤⾄ 270 亿美元，超过 7 ⽉那轮 210 亿美元的技术性抛售。与此同时， VVIX 单⽇变动幅
度为 2018 年 2 ⽉ 5 ⽇ " 波动率末⽇ " 以来最⼤。

⾼盛 FICC 与股票业务负责⼈ Rich Privorotsky 在上述⽇更中明确表态，倾向于将本轮下跌定性为
类似 1998 年的流动性危机，⽽⾮ 2000 年科技泡沫破裂的前兆。他的核⼼判断是：AI 增⻓引擎本
⾝可能依然完好，市场剧烈重定价的是建设融资与时间节奏，⽽⾮底层需求。这⼀判断对投资
者的操作含义⾄关重要 —— 若类⽐成⽴，历史上此类插曲最终都演变为买⼊机会。
杠杆⾃我强化，⽽⾮估值重估
本轮抛售的机制特征指向强制去杠杆，⽽⾮基本⾯驱动的估值重估。
Privorotsky 指出，杠杆 ETF 在收盘前的强制平仓放⼤了半导体与纳斯达克的跌幅，叠加做市商负
gamma 敞⼝形成⾃我强化的清算循环。从资⾦流向看，⾼盛主经纪业务数据显⽰，半导体板块
的核⼼压⼒在于⻓线机构（ LO ）基础需求的缺席，多策略基⾦、量化基⾦、⻓线机构与散⼾呈
现同向卖出， " 所有⼈站在同⼀边 " 本⾝即是问题所在。
相关性上升、动量因⼦跑输、残余杠杆因⼦持续抛售，⽽低⻉塔、防御与质量资产获得追捧
—— 这⼀组合读数与典型的强制降⻛险特征⾼度吻合。从选举后两⽇的市场演变来看，第⼀⽇ AI

股跑输⽽周期股因政治中⼼化预期反弹，第⼆⽇则是剧烈的补跌：若数据中⼼建设⼤幅放缓，
名义增⻓预期将⾯临下修，周期股的反弹本⾝便存在重新定价的压⼒。
1998 还是 2000 ：两种模板，两种结局
Privorotsky 将本轮下跌与两个历史节点并排⽐较，最终倾向于 1998 年模板。
⽀持 1998 年类⽐的理由在于：当年俄罗斯违约与⻓期资本管理公司（ LTCM ）危机是外部冲击，
但美国经济增⻓本⾝保持完好；⼀旦流动性恢复、美联储转向宽松，市场迅速收复失地。对应
到当下， AI 增⻓逻辑可能同样完好，市场重定价的是建设融资与推进节奏。
此外，当前科技领导者拥有真实收⼊、现⾦流与资产负债表，这与 2000 年泡沫时期的公司有本
质区别；推迟数据中⼼建设并不消灭算⼒需求，受限产能反⽽可能改善存量资产的经济性。
与 2000 年最相似的不适感在于⽀出规模的庞⼤以及回报的不确定性。核⼼问题是：市场正在清
算⼀个健全的增⻓故事，还是正在发现其经济逻辑本⾝存在缺陷？ Privorotsky 倾向于前者，但

### Page 2

强调需求、利⽤率与回报数据必须保持稳固。
AI 建设四类担忧，市场抓错了重点
⾼盛梳理了⽀撑本轮抛售的四类市场担忧：⼀是资本不⾜以⽀撑建设规模；⼆是收⼊不⾜以证
明投资合理；三是电⼒与许可容量不⾜以⽀持算⼒扩张；四是总体⽀出轨迹不可持续。
Privorotsky 认为，市场⽬前最关注的是第三条 —— 电⼒与许可问题，但这恰恰是四类担忧中最
不重要的⼀条。供给受限并不等于需求消失。真正需要回答的问题是：收⼊端能否最终证明所
投⼊资本的合理性。
在政治变量⽅⾯，得州候任州⻓ Gina Hinojosa 的表态⽐市场解读的更为温和。她明确表⽰： " 我
们可以实现经济发展，同时保护得克萨斯州⺠众，这两者并不互斥 " ，并重申 " 我并⾮憎恨数据中
⼼ " 。 Privorotsky 认为，这更像是监管层⾯的重置，⽽⾮意识形态上的拒绝，实际⽀出或将好于
当前市场折价所隐含的预期。
技术位与买点前提
从技术⾯看，纳斯达克已跌破 200 ⽇均线，标普 500 则刚好守在该均线上⽅，这⼀⽀撑位能否守
住⾄关重要。负 gamma 环境意味着市场在任⼀⽅向均存在剧烈波动的空间。
Privorotsky 在质量偏好上明确点名台积电与阿斯⻨，认为在当前不确定性下应向⾼质量科技资
产倾斜。
他同时列出改变市场叙事所需的三项条件：AI 投资形成政治正反馈、融资市场企稳，以及底层
⽀出与需求保持完好的实证证据。反证条件同样清晰：若 AI 资本开⽀遭到实质性推迟且收⼊端证
伪投资逻辑， 1998 年类⽐失效，需转向 2000 年模板；若融资市场率先出现再融资失败，下跌性
质将发⽣根本改变。
从更⻓周期看， Privorotsky 援引历史数据指出， 1950 年以来 19 次中期选举后 12 个⽉，标普 500
全部录得上涨 —— 但他强调，这是 12 个⽉维度的观察，⽽⾮⼀个⽉的预测，短期择时依然困
难。能源⽅⾯，油价单⽇反弹约 4% ，市场此前对伊朗局势快速缓和的定价过于乐观，谈判尚未
解决，能源冲击对利率、增⻓预期与⻛险偏好的复杂化影响仍是稳定化进程的重要变量。

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

A) Cooling wage pressures ease cyclical inflation (housing, food services and accommodation,
etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.
Investment Implications
While some key variables are
complicating the macro backdrop
(i.e., higher-for-longer yields/oil)
the combination of strong earnings
growth, robust consumer demand
(real final sales to private domestic
purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and
ongoing capital investment suggests
that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.
Exhibit 3: Pulse Check on: Equity/Bond Volatility and Diesel/Gasoline Prices.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.
Exhibit 4: U.S. Exceptionalism: Record Inflows of Portfolio and FDI Inflows.
A) Net Foreign Purchases of US Equities and Investment Funds. B) FDI Inflows to the U.S.
